Notes that do not change a pattern:

- The `level=error.*(sync|apply)` alternative also matches an error line whose `application=` value contains `sync`; it is an error line in any case.
- The argocd-server logs its failed API calls through the same interceptors (`server/server.go:812@v2.13.0`, `:964@v3.2.0`), so a UI action against an unreachable repository produces a second `rpc error: code = … desc = …` line in the server log.
- `ERROR_RE` counts the Info audit line `Sync operation to … failed: …` as an error line while `NOT_ERROR_RE` excludes it (`level=info`); the signature counts it regardless.

Corrections found in source:

- `argocd.sync-failed` matched `sync failed`, which no version prints (only `waitSync failed: timed out`), and four Application condition texts that never reach the log. The pattern now matches the two Info lines a failed sync produces (`one or more objects failed to apply`, `Sync operation to <rev> failed:`), gitops-engine's `Failed to sync cluster`, `Failed to initiate auto-sync to` and an error-level line about a sync or apply in both the text and the JSON form.
- `argocd.sync-failed` spelled the level only as `level=error`; Argo CD ≥ v3.0.0 defaults to JSON (`"level":"error"`), so the alternative was silent on the k3s cluster. Both spellings are in the pattern now.
- `argocd.repo-access` matched `repository not accessible` (a condition), `Unable to resolve.*revision` (the source is lowercase) and only `code = Unknown` (the repo-server's own code is Internal). The pattern now matches the lowercase texts, `failed to list refs`, both gRPC codes, go-git's `authentication required` and git's `Authentication failed for`.
- `argocd.rate-limit` matched `rate limit`, which the start-up Info line `Using default workqueue rate limiter config` contains (a false warn after five restarts), and `too many requests` / `status 429`, which nothing prints. The pattern now matches go-github's two throttle messages, `secondary rate limit`, and git's `HTTP 429` / `returned error: 429`.

### External Secrets Operator

Versions read: v0.9.13 (EKS), v2.1.0 (k3s), v2.14.0 (newest application tag; the newest release is `helm-chart-2.14.0`, 2026-10-23); boundary tags v0.10.0, v0.11.0, v0.12.0, v0.14.0, v0.16.0, v0.18.0, v0.20.0, v1.0.0, v2.0.0 (`pkg/controllers/externalsecret/externalsecret_controller.go` only). Libraries at the pins: controller-runtime v0.17.1 / v0.23.1 / v0.25.2; zap v1.26.0 / v1.27.0 / v1.28.0; apimachinery v0.29.1 / v0.35.0 / v0.37.1; aws-sdk-go v1.50.18 (v0.9.13); aws-sdk-go-v2 v1.39.6 with smithy-go v1.23.2 (`providers/v1/aws/go.mod@v2.1.0`) and v1.47.3 with smithy-go v1.28.6 (v2.14.0).

How an External Secrets line looks. controller-runtime zap JSON: `zap.New(zap.UseFlagOptions(&opts))` with `Level` and `TimeEncoder` from `--loglevel` (default `info`) and `--zap-time-encoding` (default `epoch`) (`cmd/root.go:131,135,267,268@v0.9.13`; `cmd/controller/certcontroller.go:178,182@v2.1.0` called from `cmd/controller/root.go:127,357,358@v2.1.0`; `:137,372,373@v2.14.0`). Without `Development` the encoder is zap's production JSON (`kubernetes-sigs/controller-runtime pkg/log/zap/zap.go:176,191@v0.17.1`, same lines at v0.23.1 and v0.25.2): keys `ts`, `level`, `msg`, lowercase level, epoch float time (`uber-go/zap config.go:126,127,131,134,135@v1.26.0`, same at v1.27.0 and v1.28.0). Info: `{"level":"info","ts":1793777255.8,"logger":"controllers.ExternalSecret","msg":"reconciled secret","ExternalSecret":{"name":"db","namespace":"app"}}`. Error: `{"level":"error","ts":1793777255.8377078,"msg":"Reconciler error","controller":"externalsecret","controllerGroup":"external-secrets.io","controllerKind":"ExternalSecret","ExternalSecret":{"name":"db","namespace":"app"},"namespace":"app","name":"db","reconcileID":"8d0c","error":"operation error Secrets Manager: GetSecretValue, https response error StatusCode: 400, RequestID: 1a2b, api error AccessDeniedException: …"}` — the field order is the one observed on the EKS cluster; the key name `error` is zapr's and was not read (**unverified**; it is the observed key). `ERROR_RE` matches `"level":"error"`, `NOT_ERROR_RE` matches `"level":"info"`; `_ts_of` finds nothing in the container line (`ts` is epoch) and relies on the kubectl timestamp.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `external-secrets.provider-error` | When the provider call fails the reconcile returns the error and controller-runtime logs `Reconciler error` with the error text; the controller name is the lowercased Kind, `externalsecret` | `kubernetes-sigs/controller-runtime pkg/internal/controller/controller.go:329@v0.17.1`, `:495@v0.23.1`, `:497@v0.25.2`; `pkg/builder/controller.go:339@v0.17.1`, `:387@v0.23.1`, `:387@v0.25.2`; `pkg/controllers/externalsecret/externalsecret_controller.go:209,210,632@v0.9.13`, `:401,402,1271@v2.1.0`, `:417,418,1424@v2.14.0` | all three |
| | `markAsFailed` logs `could not get secret data from provider` with `log.Error(err, msg)` up to v0.10.0; from v0.11.0 it only sets the condition and the event (and from v2.14.0 appends a safe detail to the message) | `externalsecret_controller.go:64,333@v0.9.13`, `:357@v0.10.0`, `:577@v0.11.0`, `:84,802-807@v2.1.0`, `:86,800-810@v2.14.0` | log line ≤ v0.10.0; condition only ≥ v0.11.0 |
| | `unable to create session: %w` is the AWS provider's wrap of a session/credential failure; `Secret does not exist` is `NoSecretErr`, which the Secrets Manager `fetch` returns for `ResourceNotFoundException` at v0.9.13 (v2.x maps it in other paths) | `pkg/provider/aws/provider.go:46,166@v0.9.13`, `providers/v1/aws/provider.go:49,176@v2.1.0`, `:54,203@v2.14.0`; `apis/externalsecrets/v1beta1/provider.go:103@v0.9.13`, `apis/externalsecrets/v1/provider.go:110@v2.1.0`, `:110@v2.14.0`; `pkg/provider/aws/secretsmanager/secretsmanager.go:148-151@v0.9.13`, `providers/v1/aws/secretsmanager/secretsmanager.go:184,233,255@v2.1.0`, `:188,246,268@v2.14.0` | all three, inside `"error"` |
| | AWS error codes reach the `"error"` field as `<Code>: <message>` (aws-sdk-go v1) or `operation error <Service>: <Op>, https response error StatusCode: <n>, RequestID: <id>, api error <Code>: <message>` (aws-sdk-go-v2 + smithy-go) | `aws/aws-sdk-go aws/awserr/types.go:12,13,65@v1.50.18`; `aws/smithy-go errors.go:37,60@v1.23.2`, `:37,60@v1.28.6`; `aws/aws-sdk-go-v2 aws/transport/http/response_error.go:24-26@v1.39.6`, `@v1.47.3` | the codes themselves are server side |
| | `SecretSyncedError` is a condition reason; no log call contains it | `apis/externalsecrets/v1beta1/externalsecret_types.go:425@v0.9.13`, `apis/externalsecrets/v1/externalsecret_types.go:614@v2.1.0`, `:692@v2.14.0` | dropped (condition only) |
| | `Unable to assume role` and `unable to get session` appear nowhere in the operator or in aws-sdk-go's stscreds (`failed to retrieve credentials` is its text); `ExpiredToken` only as the STS code in a comment | `aws/aws-sdk-go aws/credentials/stscreds/web_identity_provider.go:170@v1.50.18`, `assume_role_provider.go:229@v1.50.18` | dropped (**unverified** server-side texts) |
| `external-secrets.update-failed` | The `Reconciler error` line carries the apiserver's text when the Secret update fails, but it is counted once, by `external-secrets.provider-error` (one reconcile failure, one finding); this signature alone keeps apimachinery's conflict text `Operation cannot be fulfilled` inside that line (`external-secrets.provider-error` excludes it with a negative lookahead) and the operator's own `could not update Secret` (v0.9.x; `could not update secret`, lowercase, in v2.x) and `unable to patch status` are logged only up to v0.10.0 | `externalsecret_controller.go:59,60,194,307,308,333@v0.9.13`, `:357@v0.10.0`, `:577@v0.11.0`, `:87,602,603,802@v2.1.0`, `:89,582,583,800@v2.14.0` | log line ≤ v0.10.0; Reconciler error all three |
| | apimachinery's conflict text `Operation cannot be fulfilled on %s %q: %v` is in the `"error"` field when the Secret update conflicts; v2.x requeues status conflicts at V(1) without an error | `kubernetes/apimachinery pkg/api/errors/errors.go:241@v0.29.1`, `:242@v0.35.0`, `:242@v0.37.1`; `externalsecret_controller.go:378,379@v2.1.0`, `:394,395@v2.14.0` | all three |
| | `error patching` and `could not create Secret` are printed by nothing (case-insensitive grep of the controller: 0) | — | dropped |

Notes that do not change a pattern:

- Both signatures of this collector count the `Reconciler error` line; the provider-error one adds the texts that distinguish a store failure, the update-failed one the apiserver conflict text. A snapshot with 32 such lines (observed on EKS at v0.9.13) shows up under both.
- The parameter-store provider logs `GetParametersByPath: access denied. using fallback to describe parameters …` at Info when it lacks `ssm:GetParametersByPath` (`providers/v1/aws/parameterstore/parameterstore.go:398@v2.1.0`, `:393@v2.14.0`); it is lowercase and does not match.

Corrections found in source:

- `external-secrets.provider-error` relied on `could not get secret data from provider`, which the operator stopped logging at v0.11.0 (`markAsFailed` lost its `log.Error`), so the signature was silent on the k3s cluster (v2.1.0) and on every current version. The pattern now matches controller-runtime's `"msg":"Reconciler error"` line for the `externalsecret` controller, keeps the ≤ v0.10.0 line, and adds the provider texts that exist (`unable to create session`, `Secret does not exist`); `SecretSyncedError` (condition reason), `Unable to assume role`, `unable to get session` (nowhere), `ExpiredToken`, `AccessDenied`, `ResourceNotFoundException`, `AccessDeniedException` (AWS codes inside the field that the Reconciler error line already matches) were dropped.
- `external-secrets.update-failed` relied on `could not update Secret` (logged only ≤ v0.10.0, lowercase `secret` in v2.x and condition only), `error patching` and `could not create Secret` (printed by nothing). The `Reconciler error` line is counted by `external-secrets.provider-error` alone (both matched it at first, which doubled every reconcile failure on the EKS cluster); this pattern keeps `could not update Secret` and adds `unable to patch status` for v0.9.x, and keeps `Operation cannot be fulfilled`.

### KEDA

Versions read: v2.17.2 (EKS), v2.19.0 (k3s), v2.21.0 (newest release, 2026-09-23); no phrase changed between them. controller-runtime pins v0.19.7 (`go.mod` replace @v2.17.2), v0.22.4 (replace @v2.19.0), v0.24.1 (v2.21.0) were not fetched; the zap option code was read at v0.17.1, v0.23.1 and v0.25.2 (`pkg/log/zap/zap.go:171-221`, identical). zap v1.27.0 (indirect, v2.17.2), v1.27.1 (v2.19.0), v1.28.0 (v2.21.0); the encoder configs were read at v1.26.0, v1.27.0, v1.28.0 (identical lines). kedacore/charts v2.17.2 and v2.19.0.

How a KEDA line looks. `cmd/operator/main.go` binds controller-runtime's zap flags and builds the logger from them (`:113,114,118@v2.17.2`, `:115,116,120@v2.19.0`, `:136,137,156@v2.21.0`); the binary alone would print production JSON, but every shipped install passes `--zap-log-level=info --zap-encoder=console --zap-time-encoding=rfc3339` (`config/manager/manager.yaml:35-37@v2.17.2`, `@v2.19.0`, `@v2.21.0`; chart `keda/templates/manager/deployment.yaml:83-85` from `logging.operator.{level: info, format: console, timeEncoding: rfc3339}`, `keda/values.yaml:380,383,386@v2.17.2`, `:411,414,417@v2.19.0`). The console encoder (`kubernetes-sigs/controller-runtime pkg/log/zap/zap.go:178`) uses zap's development config: uppercase level (`uber-go/zap config.go:211@v1.26.0`), tab separator (`zapcore/console_encoder.go:61,106@v1.26.0`), the time per the flag. Info: `2026-11-03T15:12:27Z	INFO	cert-rotation	Ensuring CA cert	{"name": "keda-admission"}`; error: `2026-11-03T15:12:27Z	ERROR	scale_handler	error getting metric for trigger	{"trigger": "prometheus", "error": "…"}`. `ERROR_RE` matches the error line on `ERROR` and `"error"`; `NOT_ERROR_RE` has no clause for console `INFO`, so an Info line is classified by the absence of error words — the sample above has none; an Info line whose message contains `error` or `failed` (the V(1) `scaler error encountered, clearing scaler cache` when debug logging is on) would be counted as an unclassified error. `_ts_of` finds the RFC3339 time at the start of the container line.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `keda.scaler-error` | The scale handler logs `error getting scalers cache`, `error getting metric for trigger`, `error getting metric spec for the scaler`, `error getting metric source`, `error getting scale decision` (lowercase) at Error, the scaler error in the trailing `"error"` field | `pkg/scaling/scale_handler.go:481,554,747,778,781,823@v2.17.2`, `:460,538,825,869,872,912@v2.19.0`, `:197,679,815,1135,1194,1197,1237@v2.21.0` | all three, `--zap-log-level=info` |
| | `failed to get metrics …` (lowercase) is the Azure Data Explorer / Log Analytics scalers' error text, inside that field | `pkg/scalers/azure_data_explorer_scaler.go:182@v2.17.2`, `:182@v2.19.0`, `:155@v2.21.0`; `azure_log_analytics_scaler.go:270@v2.17.2`, `:209@v2.19.0`, `:196@v2.21.0` | all three |
| | `unauthorized to connect to Datadog …` exists from v2.19.0; IBM MQ prints `authentication failed: incorrect username or password` on a 401, Elasticsearch forecast `elasticsearch authentication failed (HTTP 401): …` (v2.21.0) | `pkg/scalers/datadog_scaler.go:314@v2.19.0`, `:317@v2.21.0`; `ibmmq_scaler.go:169@v2.17.2`, `:159@v2.19.0`, `:158@v2.21.0`; `elastic_forecast_scaler.go:233@v2.21.0` | per scaler |
| | A missing TriggerAuthentication prints `TriggerAuthentication Not Found` (Info) followed by `Failed to get TriggerAuthentication` (Error); the cluster-scoped controller prints `Failed to get ClusterTriggerAuthentication` | `pkg/status/status.go:119,121@v2.17.2`, `:121,123@v2.19.0`, `:121,123@v2.21.0`; `controllers/keda/clustertriggerauthentication_controller.go:72@v2.17.2`, `:72@v2.19.0`, `:73@v2.21.0` | all three |
| | `scaler error encountered, clearing scaler cache` and `Getting metrics and activity from scaler … "scalerError"` are V(1) (debug) lines; `… please use ClusterTriggerAuthentication instead of TriggerAuthentication` is V(1) too | `pkg/scaling/scale_handler.go:636,756,842@v2.19.0`; `pkg/scaling/resolver/scale_resolvers.go:71@v2.17.2`, `:86@v2.19.0`, `:155@v2.21.0` | not printed at the shipped level (reason the broad `scaler.*error` is anchored to `\tERROR\t` and `TriggerAuthentication` to the two error lines) |
| | A scaler failure also raises the Event `KEDAScalerFailed`; Events are not log lines | `pkg/eventreason/eventreason.go:90@v2.19.0`; `pkg/scaling/scalers_builder.go:95@v2.19.0`, `scale_handler.go:539,545@v2.19.0` | event only |

Notes that do not change a pattern:

- `error getting (scaler|metric)` also matches `error getting scalers cache …` (the `s` follows the group).
- `Reconciling ScaledObject` (`controllers/keda/scaledobject_controller.go:172@v2.19.0`) is the normal Info line of the operator; it contains neither `scaler` nor an error word.

Corrections found in source:

- `keda.scaler-error` spelled `Failed to get metrics` and `Error getting scale decision` with a capital; the source prints `failed to get metrics` and `error getting scale decision`, so both were silent. They are lowercase now.
- `keda.scaler-error` matched `unauthorized`, which only the Datadog scaler prints from v2.19.0; the pattern now also matches `Unauthorized`, `authentication failed` and `HTTP 401`.
- `keda.scaler-error` matched `scaler.*error` and `TriggerAuthentication` anywhere; at the shipped level the only matching lines are error contexts, but with debug logging the V(1) lines above would count. The pattern anchors the first to the console `ERROR` column and narrows the second to `Failed to get (Cluster)?TriggerAuthentication` / `TriggerAuthentication Not Found`.

### cert-manager

Versions read: v1.14.4 (k3s), v1.21.3 (newest release, 2026-10-23); boundary tags v1.15.0, v1.16.0, v1.17.0, v1.18.0, v1.19.0, v1.20.0, v1.21.0, v1.21.1, v1.21.2 (`pkg/controller/acmechallenges/sync.go` only). Libraries at the pins: klog v2.110.1 / v2.140.0; component-base v0.29.0 / v0.36.2; apimachinery v0.29.0 / v0.36.2; golang.org/x/crypto v0.17.0 (the ACME client of v1.14.4, `pkg/acme/client/interfaces.go:24@v1.14.4`) and the in-tree `third_party/forked/acme` at v1.21.3; the Helm chart in `deploy/charts/cert-manager`.

How a cert-manager line looks. klog text through logr: `logf.Log = klog.TODO().WithName("cert-manager")` (`pkg/logs/logs.go:40@v1.14.4`, `:38@v1.21.3`); the flags come from component-base's `LoggingConfiguration` (`logs.go:83@v1.14.4`, `:70@v1.21.3`), `--logging-format` defaults to `text` (`kubernetes/component-base logs/api/v1/types.go:30@v0.29.0`) with `json` registered (`logs.go:33@v1.14.4`, `:35@v1.21.3`). The chart passes `--v=2` (`deploy/charts/cert-manager/values.yaml:46@v1.14.4`, `:71@v1.21.3`; `templates/deployment.yaml:85@v1.14.4`, `:94@v1.21.3`; `templates/cainjector-deployment.yaml:67@v1.14.4`, `:84@v1.21.3`); `logf.DebugLevel` is 4 (`logs.go:50@v1.14.4`, `:47@v1.21.3`), so `log.V(logf.DebugLevel).Info` lines never print at the default and `log.Error` always does. The header is `[IWEF]mmdd hh:mm:ss.uuuuuu threadid file:line] ` (`kubernetes/klog internal/buffer/buffer.go:117,133@v2.110.1`, `:117@v2.140.0`), an error adds `err="…"` (`klog.go:809@v2.110.1`, `:848@v2.140.0`). Info: `I1103 16:09:06.220620       1 reconciler.go:142] "Updated object" logger="cert-manager" kind="Deployment"`; error: `E1103 16:09:06.220620       1 indexers.go:67] "unable to fetch certificate that owns the secret" err="certificates.cert-manager.io \"web-tls\" not found" logger="cert-manager" secret="app/web-tls"`. With the kubectl prefix the error line matches `ERROR_RE`'s klog clause `^\[?[^\]]*\]? ?\S* ?E\d{4} `; the Info line matches nothing in either regex. `_ts_of` finds the klog header time.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cert-manager.issue-failed` | `error issuing certificate request` (certificaterequests) and the acmeorders Error lines `failed to create Order resource due to bad request, marking Order as failed`, `failed to retrieve the ACME order (4xx error) marking Order as failed`, `failed to update Order status due to a 4xx error, marking Order as failed`, `failed to finalize Order resource due to bad request, marking Order as failed`, and from v1.21 `acme server fatal error` | `pkg/controller/certificaterequests/sync.go:143@v1.14.4`, `:143@v1.21.3`; `pkg/controller/acmeorders/sync.go:102,184,208,248,290,544,562,573@v1.14.4`, `:106,188,212,247,333,592,610,617,625@v1.21.3` | both, `--v=2` |
| | An ACME problem document renders as `<status> <type>: <detail>` (`"%d %s: %s"`), so the `err=` value holds `urn:ietf:params:acme:error:<type>`; the `rateLimited` type and the status 429 are the ACME server's (RFC 8555 §6.7), **unverified** in any repository beyond the prefix constant | `golang/crypto acme/types.go:112@v0.17.0`; `cert-manager/cert-manager third_party/forked/acme/types.go:135@v1.21.3`; `pkg/issuer/acme/setup.go:75@v1.21.3` | both |
| | `failed to issue`, `Error getting certificate`, `acme.*level=error` (klog has no `level=`), `acme.*status 429` (the number comes first) are printed by nothing; `failed to request token for %s/%s: %w` is a v1.21 DNS-01 token error | `pkg/issuer/acme/dns/dns.go:572@v1.21.3` | dropped |
| `cert-manager.challenge-failed` | `propagation check failed` is logged at Error on every failed self-check up to v1.20.x; from v1.21.0 the readiness evaluator returns the reason and the controller logs `challenge not ready to accept yet` at V(4) only | `pkg/controller/acmechallenges/sync.go:190@v1.14.4` (also 1 hit at v1.15.0–v1.19.0), `:187@v1.20.0`; `:209@v1.21.0`, `:209@v1.21.3`; `readiness.go:56@v1.21.3` | ≤ v1.20.x |
| | `error getting solver for challenge`, `error cleaning up challenge`, `error accepting challenge`, `error waiting for authorization` are Error lines; `Accepting challenge authorization failed: %v` is an Event (reason Failed) | `sync.go:106,114,274,282,371,379,404@v1.14.4`, `:361,372,461,477,502@v1.21.3` | both; the Event text is not in the pattern (see note) |
| | `Waiting for %s challenge propagation: %s` is the Challenge status reason at every version, never a log line | `sync.go:191@v1.14.4`; `readiness.go:56@v1.21.3` | dropped (status only) |
| `cert-manager.orphan-tls-secret` | The cainjector's Secret-to-Certificate indexer logs `unable to fetch certificate that owns the secret` at Error for any `Get` error (source comment `TODO: check for not found error?`), once per Secret event; a missing Certificate renders as `certificates.cert-manager.io "<name>" not found` in `err=` | `pkg/controller/cainjector/indexers.go:61@v1.14.4`, `:67@v1.21.3`; `kubernetes/apimachinery pkg/api/errors/errors.go:154@v0.29.0`, `:155@v0.36.2`; `kubernetes/klog klog.go:809@v2.110.1`, `:848@v2.140.0` | both |

Notes that do not change a pattern:

- `Accepting challenge authorization failed` is passed to `Eventf`, not to the logger (`sync.go:404@v1.14.4`, `:502@v1.21.3`); client-go records Events in the log only at `klog.V(3)`, so at `--v=2` this alternative matches nothing and only the four Error lines count.
- The orphan count is events, not Secrets: one orphan Secret re-indexed on every cainjector resync produces many lines (64 in 24 hours on the k3s cluster).

Corrections found in source:

- `cert-manager.challenge-failed` relied on `propagation check failed`, which v1.21.0 removed (the wait became a V(4) line and a status reason), and on `Waiting for (HTTP-01|DNS-01) challenge`, which was always a status reason. The pattern keeps `propagation check failed` for ≤ v1.20.x and now names the solver, cleanup, accept and authorization Error lines instead of the broad `challenge.*(failed|error)`.
- `cert-manager.issue-failed` matched `failed to issue`, `Error getting certificate`, `acme.*level=error` and `acme.*status 429`, none of which any version prints. The pattern now names the certificaterequests and acmeorders Error lines and keeps the ACME URN prefix (which covers `rateLimited`).
- `cert-manager.orphan-tls-secret` matched every `unable to fetch certificate that owns the secret` line, including transient apiserver errors. It now requires `not found` in the same line.

### Prometheus

Versions read: v2.49.1 (the EKS cluster), v3.7.3 (the k3s cluster), v3.15.1 (newest release). Logging libraries at the pins of each `go.mod`: `prometheus/common` promlog v0.45.0 (`go.mod:50@v2.49.1`), promslog v0.67.1 (`go.mod:59@v3.7.3`) and v0.71.0 (`go.mod:69@v3.15.1`); go-kit/log v0.2.1 (`go.mod:21@v2.49.1`). Go strings were read at go1.26.10 (`go.mod` `go 1.26.0`@v3.15.1; the ENOSPC string is identical in the go1.24.13 tree).

How a Prometheus line looks. v2.49.1 writes go-kit logfmt through promlog: `ts=2026-11-05T10:05:50.123Z caller=main.go:1 level=info msg="..."` — `log.With(l, "ts", timestampFormat, "caller", ...)` inside `level.NewFilter` and the level prefixed outside it (`promlog/log.go:33-36,125-126@v0.45.0`; go-kit `log.go:52-66@v0.2.1` `WithPrefix`), level words lower-case (`level/level.go:233-237@v0.2.1`), `--log.level` default `info`, `--log.format` default `logfmt` (`promlog/flag/flag.go:40,44@v0.45.0`). v3.x writes Go `log/slog` text through promslog with the default slog style (`promslog.New` `cmd/prometheus/main.go:590@v3.7.3`, `:680@v3.15.1`; no `Style` set, `promslog/slog.go:275-282@v0.71.0`): `time=2026-11-05T10:05:50.123+00:00 level=INFO source=main.go:1 msg="..."` — `AddSource: true` and `defaultReplaceAttr` (`promslog/slog.go:265-272,214-226@v0.71.0`), time as RFC3339 with milliseconds (`src/log/slog/handler.go:614-622@go1.26.10`), level `INFO|WARN|ERROR|DEBUG` (`src/log/slog/level.go:73-79@go1.26.10`), defaults `info`/`logfmt` (`promslog/flag/flag.go:46-52@v0.71.0`). `ERROR_RE`/`NOT_ERROR_RE` (`lib/logs.py:34-37`) are case-insensitive, so `level=info msg="Server is ready to receive web requests."` and `level=INFO ...` are not errors, and `level=error msg="Scrape commit failed" err="..."` / `level=ERROR ...` are; a `level=warn`/`WARN` line with "failed" in it (`Evaluating rule failed`, `Append failed`) is an unclassified error unless a signature consumes it.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `prometheus.rule-eval-failed` | The rule manager logs `Evaluating rule failed` rule=... err=... at Warn when a rule's query fails | `rules/group.go:462@v2.49.1`, `:543@v3.7.3`, `:548@v3.15.1` | all three, default level |
| | `rule evaluation failed` and `Error evaluating rule` are printed by no version (grepped case-insensitively over the three trees) | as listed | — |
| `prometheus.wal-corrupt` | A corrupt WAL prints `Encountered WAL read error, attempting repair` (Warn) with `err="corruption in segment <file> at <offset>: ..."`, then `Starting corruption repair` (Warn) and `Successfully repaired WAL` (Info); corrupt head chunk files print `corruption in head chunk file`; the legacy WAL migration prints `WAL corruption detected; truncating` | `tsdb/db.go:916,920@v2.49.1`, `:1019,1023@v3.7.3`, `:1219,1223@v3.15.1`; `tsdb/wlog/wlog.go:116,424@v2.49.1`, `:116,413@v3.7.3`, `:116,413@v3.15.1`; `tsdb/chunks/head_chunks.go:111@v2.49.1`, `:110@v3.7.3`, `:111@v3.15.1`; `tsdb/wal.go:250@v2.49.1` (removed at 3.x) | all three |
| | The startup Warn `This filesystem is not supported and may lead to data corruption and data loss` is printed when the data directory is on an unsupported filesystem; it is not corruption and the pattern now excludes it | `cmd/prometheus/main.go:1058,1114@v2.49.1`, `:1312,1368@v3.7.3`, `:1518,1580@v3.15.1` | all three |
| `prometheus.low-disk` | ENOSPC reaches the log as the Go error text `no space left on device` inside `err=` of the failing write (for instance `Scrape commit failed`) | `src/syscall/zerrors_linux_amd64.go:1385@go1.26.10`; `scrape/scrape.go:1225@v2.49.1`, `:1427@v3.7.3`, `:1528@v3.15.1` | all three |
| | `low disk space` and `out of disk` are printed by no version | — | — |
| `prometheus.scrape-error` | At the default level the scrape loop logs `Scrape commit failed` (Error), `Append failed` and `Appending scrape report failed` (Warn, stale-marker and report appends) | `scrape/scrape.go:1225,1231,1241,1295@v2.49.1`, `:1427,1433,1443,1502@v3.7.3`, `:1528,1541,1551,1629@v3.15.1` | all three, default level |
| | Per-target failures (`Scrape failed` err=...) and the per-target `Append failed` are Debug only; the error text carries a `Get "url":` prefix and, for HTTP errors, the reason phrase (`server returned HTTP status 503 Service Unavailable`) | `scrape/scrape.go:1274,1289,712@v2.49.1`, `:1476,1496,852@v3.7.3`, `:1594,1623,851@v3.15.1`; `promlog/flag/flag.go:40@v0.45.0`, `promslog/flag/flag.go:47@v0.71.0` | all three |
| `prometheus.out-of-order` | Per scrape, one Warn summary per kind: `Error on ingesting out-of-order samples`, `... samples with different value but same timestamp`, `... samples that are too old or are too far into the future` (num_dropped=N); the rules path prints `Error on ingesting out-of-order result from rule evaluation` | `scrape/scrape.go:1655,1658,1661@v2.49.1`, `:1920,1923,1926@v3.7.3`, `:2077,2080,2083@v3.15.1`; `rules/group.go:528@v2.49.1`, `:610@v3.7.3`, `:633@v3.15.1` | all three, default level |
| | The per-series lines (`Out of order sample`, `Duplicate sample for timestamp`, `Out of bounds metric`) are Debug; the lower-case error constants `out of order sample`, `out of bounds`, `duplicate sample for timestamp` reach the log at Error only in the remote-write receiver (`Out of order sample from remote write` err=...) | `scrape/scrape.go:1695,1700,1705@v2.49.1`, `:2024,2029,2034@v3.7.3`, `:2182,2187,2192@v3.15.1`; `storage/interface.go:34,36,40@v2.49.1` (same lines at v3.7.3, v3.15.1; at 3.x the duplicate error is a struct type whose `Error()` text is **unverified**, `storage/interface.go:53-60@v3.15.1`); `storage/remote/write_handler.go:132@v2.49.1`, `:328@v3.7.3`, `:230@v3.15.1` | all three |

Notes that do not change a pattern:

- `Successfully repaired WAL` is Info and belongs to the same corruption event as the two Warn lines; one corruption counts two or three lines.
- Prometheus 3.x `--log.format=json` writes `{"time":"...","level":"INFO","source":"...","msg":"..."}`; both classification regexes still see `"level":"INFO"`.

Corrections found in source:

- `prometheus.scrape-error` could never match: its `err="context deadline exceeded"`, `err="connection refused"` and `err="server returned HTTP status 5\d\d"` alternatives were anchored to whole `err=` values, but the only lines carrying them are Debug (`scrape/scrape.go:1274@v2.49.1`, `:1594@v3.15.1`) and their values start with `Get "<url>":` or end with the reason phrase. `scrape.*error` matched only the 2.x key order. The pattern now matches the default-level lines `Scrape commit failed`, `Append failed` and `Appending scrape report failed`; target health is read from `prometheus/targets.json`.
- `prometheus.out-of-order` matched only the remote-write receiver's Error line; for scraped samples the phrases are Debug. The pattern now also matches the Warn summaries `Error on ingesting ...` (`scrape/scrape.go:1655-1661@v2.49.1`, `:2077-2083@v3.15.1`).
- `prometheus.wal-corrupt`'s bare `corruption` matched the startup filesystem warning (`cmd/prometheus/main.go:1058@v2.49.1`, `:1518@v3.15.1`), a false critical on NFS or overlay data directories; it is now `corruption (in|repair|detected)`.
- `prometheus.rule-eval-failed` lost `rule evaluation failed` and `Error evaluating rule`, `prometheus.low-disk` lost `low disk space` and `out of disk`: no version prints them.

### Alertmanager

Versions read: v0.26.0 (EKS), v0.29.0 (k3s), v0.34.2 (newest), plus v0.27.0 and v0.28.0 for the logging boundary. Libraries: go-kit/log v0.2.1 and `prometheus/common` promlog v0.44.0 (`go.mod:12,33@v0.26.0`), promslog v0.67.1 (`go.mod:38@v0.29.0`) and v0.70.1 (`go.mod:40@v0.34.2`); hashicorp/memberlist v0.5.0 (`go.mod:25@v0.26.0`), v0.5.3 (`go.mod:31@v0.29.0`), v0.6.0 (`go.mod:33@v0.34.2`); hashicorp/go-multierror v1.1.1 (memberlist `go.mod:9@v0.6.0`).

How an Alertmanager line looks. v0.26.0 and v0.27.0 use promlog like Prometheus 2.x: `ts=... caller=... level=warn msg="..."` (`promlog/log.go:125-128@v0.44.0`; `cmd/alertmanager/main.go:243@v0.26.0`, `:179@v0.27.0`). v0.28.0 switched to promslog (`cmd/alertmanager/main.go:189@v0.28.0`, `:190@v0.29.0`, `:102@v0.34.2`): `time=... level=WARN source=cluster.go:271 msg="..." component=cluster` (attributes from `logger.With` come after `msg`). Classification is as for Prometheus: `level=info msg="gossip settled; proceeding"` is not an error; `level=error msg="Notify for alerts failed" ...` is.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `alertmanager.notify-failed` | After the retries the dispatcher logs `Notify for alerts failed` at Error (Debug only when the context was cancelled by a reload) with `err="<receiver>/<integration>[n]: notify retry canceled after N attempts: ..."`; each failed attempt before that logs `Notify attempt failed, will retry later` at Warn | `dispatch/dispatch.go:352@v0.26.0`, `:361,363@v0.29.0`, `:587,589@v0.34.2`; `notify/notify.go:728,741,745,765@v0.26.0`, `:846,863,868,895@v0.29.0`; `notify/retry_stage.go:143,160,165@v0.34.2` | all three, default level |
| | `Error on notify` is printed by no version; `failed to send reliable` is a Debug line of the gossip channel | `cluster/channel.go:112@v0.26.0`, `:115@v0.29.0`, `:117@v0.34.2` | — |
| `alertmanager.cluster-error` | A failed startup join logs `failed to join cluster` (Warn) and `unable to join gossip mesh` (Warn); a failed mesh init logs `unable to initialize gossip mesh` (Error; at v0.34.2 it is the wrapped error of `alertmanager exited with error`); an unroutable advertise address logs `this node will be unreachable in the cluster` under `component=cluster` (at v0.26.0 the text is the value of an `err` key, there is no `msg`) | `cluster/cluster.go:263,182@v0.26.0`, `:262,181@v0.29.0`, `:271,188@v0.34.2`; `cmd/alertmanager/main.go:278,348,262@v0.26.0`, `:260,334,244@v0.29.0`; `app/app.go:234,354@v0.34.2`, `cmd/alertmanager/main.go:207@v0.34.2` | all three, default level |
| | The join error text is go-multierror's `1 error occurred:\n\t* <err>` around memberlist's `Failed to join <addr>: <err>` (lower-case `failed to join` from v0.6.0) | `hashicorp/go-multierror format.go:16@v1.1.1`; `hashicorp/memberlist memberlist.go:283@v0.5.0`, `:286@v0.6.0` | all three |
| | memberlist's own `[ERR] memberlist: ...` lines never reach the default log: v0.26.0 writes them through a `logWriter` at `level.Debug`, v0.28.0 and later through `slog.NewLogLogger(handler, slog.LevelDebug)` | `cluster/cluster.go:228,340-342@v0.26.0`, `:227@v0.29.0`, `:236@v0.34.2`; `memberlist net.go:247-338@v0.5.0`, `:254-351@v0.6.0` | all three |

Notes that do not change a pattern:

- `Notify for alerts failed` and `notify retry canceled` are the same line (the latter is its `err=` value), so one failed notification counts once plus one `Notify attempt failed` line per retry.
- The `cluster.*unreachable` alternative matches at v0.28.0+ because `source=cluster.go:NNN` precedes the message; at v0.26.0 because `component=cluster` precedes `err=`.

Corrections found in source:

- `alertmanager.notify-failed` did not count the per-retry Warn `Notify attempt failed, will retry later` (`notify/notify.go:745@v0.26.0`, `retry_stage.go:165@v0.34.2`), so a receiver that was down for an hour showed one line; the pattern now matches it. `Error on notify` and the Debug-only `failed to send` were removed.
- `alertmanager.cluster-error`'s `gossip.*(error|failed)` matched only through the multierror text inside `err=` of `unable to join gossip mesh`; the pattern now names that line and `unable to initialize gossip mesh`. Runtime gossip failures are Debug-only (`cluster/cluster.go:227@v0.29.0`), so the signature can only report startup failures; the meaning says so.

### Grafana

Versions read: v10.2.3 (EKS), v12.2.1 (k3s), v13.3.0 (newest). SQLite drivers: mattn/go-sqlite3 v1.14.16 (`go.mod:85@v10.2.3`) and v1.14.22 (`go.mod:136@v12.2.1`), used with cgo (`pkg/build/cmd.go:285@v10.2.3`, `:311@v12.2.1` set `CGO_ENABLED=1`; `pkg/util/sqlite/sqlite_cgo.go:1,8@v12.2.1`); modernc.org/sqlite v1.58.0 at v13.3.0 (`pkg/util/sqlite/go.mod:5@v13.3.0`, the only driver file is `sqlite_nocgo.go`), read from the module zip of proxy.golang.org (the project lives at gitlab.com/cznic/sqlite). go-kit/log v0.2.1 (`go.mod:64@v12.2.1`).

How a Grafana line looks. go-kit through `pkg/infra/log`: `[log] mode = console` writes logfmt unless stdout is a TTY (`pkg/infra/log/log.go:366-381@v10.2.3`, `:385-400@v12.2.1`; `pkg/infra/log/text/text_logger.go:34@v12.2.1` is `gokitlog.NewLogfmtLogger`), `format = json` uses `gokitlog.NewJSONLogger` (`:379@v10.2.3`, `:398@v12.2.1`). The named logger is `With(root, "logger", name)` swapped into a `SwapLogger` (`log.go:165,244@v10.2.3`, `:182,263@v12.2.1`), and each call is `With(&SwapLogger, "t", RFC3339Nano).Log(level.Key(), lvl, "msg", msg, args...)` (`:187,200@v10.2.3`, `:204,217@v12.2.1`, `logTimeFormat` `:34`/`:37`), so the line reads `logger=provisioning.dashboard t=2026-11-05T10:05:50.123456789Z level=error msg="failed to save dashboard" file=... error="..."`; level words are go-kit's lower-case (`level/level.go:233-237@v0.2.1`). `ERROR_RE`/`NOT_ERROR_RE` classify `... level=info msg="Request Completed" ...` as not an error and `... level=error msg="..."` as an error; the JSON form `{"level":"info",...}` works the same.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `grafana.db-locked` | SQLite's texts for SQLITE_BUSY and SQLITE_LOCKED are `database is locked` and `database table is locked`; modernc formats them as `database is locked (5) (SQLITE_BUSY)` | `mattn/go-sqlite3 sqlite3-binding.c:172288,172289@v1.14.16`, `:179751,179752@v1.14.22`; `cznic/sqlite lib/sqlite_g_0000000000000003.go:10059@v1.58.0`, `conn.go:869-891@v1.58.0` | all three |
| | Grafana logs the text at **Info** as `Database locked, sleeping then retrying` error=... retry=N and retries (up to five times) | `pkg/services/sqlstore/transactions.go:74@v10.2.3`, `:72@v12.2.1`, `:94@v13.3.0`; `pkg/services/sqlstore/session.go:96@v10.2.3`, `:88@v12.2.1`, `:88@v13.3.0` | all three, default level |
| `grafana.provisioning-error` | Logger names `provisioning`, `provisioning.dashboard`, `provisioning.datasources`, `provisioning.plugins`, `provisioning.alerting` (`provisioning.notifiers` at v10 only; `provisioning.stub`, `provisioning.informer.*` from v13) | `pkg/services/provisioning/provisioning.go:114@v10.2.3`, `:130@v12.2.1`, `:120@v13.3.0`; `dashboards/dashboard.go:43@v10.2.3`, `:48@v12.2.1`, `:50@v13.3.0`; `datasources/datasources.go:38`/`:39`/`:37`; `plugins/plugin_provisioner.go:16`/`:16`/`:22`; `alerting/provisioner.go:24`/`:25`/`:25`; `notifiers/alert_notifications.go:30@v10.2.3` | all three |
| | `failed to save dashboard` and `failed to load dashboard from ` (Error), `Failed to provision data sources|plugins|alerting|dashboard` (Error) and `Failed to provision config` (Warn) | `dashboards/file_reader.go:152,181,234@v10.2.3`, `:162,192,245@v12.2.1`, `:232,277,333@v13.3.0`; `provisioning.go:153,159,165,171,181@v10.2.3`, `:195,201,207,228@v12.2.1`, `:146,151,156,169,227@v13.3.0`; `dashboards/dashboard.go:73@v10.2.3`, `:94@v12.2.1`, `:106@v13.3.0` | all three |
| | `failed to sync dashboard` is printed by no version | — | — |
| `grafana.auth-failed` | A failed login logs `Failed to authenticate request` client=... error=... at **Info**: `errorLogFunc` takes the level from the errutil status, and Unauthorized maps to Info; the error text is `[password-auth.failed] failed to authenticate identity: ...` or `[api-key.invalid] API key is invalid` | `pkg/services/authn/authnimpl/service.go:230,359-364@v10.2.3`, `:156,464-472@v12.2.1`, `:157,478-486@v13.3.0`; `pkg/util/errutil/status.go:128-129@v10.2.3`, `pkg/apimachinery/errutil/status.go:138-139@v12.2.1`, `@v13.3.0`; `pkg/util/errutil/errors.go:365@v10.2.3`, `pkg/apimachinery/errutil/errors.go:384@v12.2.1`, `@v13.3.0`; `pkg/services/authn/clients/password.go:63@v10.2.3`, `:77@v12.2.1`, `:82@v13.3.0`; `pkg/services/authn/clients/api_key.go:53@v10.2.3`, `:64@v12.2.1`, `:65@v13.3.0` | all three, default level |
| | `Invalid username or password` is the HTTP public message (`errutil.WithPublicMessage`), never logged; `Invalid credentials` appears nowhere; the store's `invalid API key` (`pkg/services/apikey/model.go:14@v10.2.3`, `:13@v12.2.1`, `:13@v13.3.0`) is replaced by the authn client before logging — whether it survives for other store errors is **unverified** (the else branch of `api_key.go:50-60` was not read) | `pkg/services/authn/clients/password.go:16@v10.2.3`, `:18@v12.2.1`, `:18@v13.3.0` | all three |

Notes that do not change a pattern:

- `Failed to authenticate password identity` is Debug (`password.go:52@v10.2.3`, `:65@v12.2.1`, `:66@v13.3.0`); the Info line of the authn service is the one counted.

Corrections found in source:

- `grafana.db-locked` counted the Info retry line with `warn_at: 1`, so one transient lock on a single-replica SQLite was reported as failing saves; `warn_at` is now 20 and `fail_at` 200, and the meaning names the retry line.
- `grafana.provisioning-error`'s `logger=provisioning.*level=(error|warn)` was silent with `[log] format = json`; the JSON twin `"logger":"provisioning..."..."level":"(error|warn)"` was added and `sync` dropped.
- `grafana.auth-failed` matched `Invalid username or password` and `Invalid credentials`, which are never logged, and `invalid API key`, which the authn client rewrites to `API key is invalid` (`api_key.go:53@v10.2.3`); the pattern now matches `Failed to authenticate` and both API-key spellings.

### Loki and promtail

Versions read: v2.6.1 (the EKS Loki), v2.9.2 (the EKS promtail image), v3.7.9 (newest Loki); v3.0.0 to v3.6.0 for `pkg/validation/validate.go` (the texts were lower-cased at v3.6.0; v3.5.0 still capitalised). promtail is still in `clients/pkg/promtail` at v3.7.9 and is gone at v4.0.0-rc.2 (`gh api repos/grafana/loki/contents/clients/pkg?ref=v4.0.0-rc.2` lists only `logentry` and `util`). Libraries: go-kit/log v0.2.0 (`go.mod:39@v2.6.1`) and v0.2.1 (`go.mod:38@v2.9.2`, `:43@v3.7.9`); grafana/tail (`go.mod:296@v2.6.1` replace, `:56@v2.9.2`, `:59@v3.7.9`); weaveworks/common and grafana/dskit read in each tag's `vendor/`; Go strings at go1.26.10 (`go.mod` `go 1.26.10`@v3.7.9).

How a Loki or promtail line looks. Both binaries share `pkg/util/log`: go-kit logfmt by default, JSON with `-log.format=json` (`pkg/util/log/log.go:46-50@v2.6.1`, `:167-171@v2.9.2`, `:164@v3.7.9`). The filter wraps the raw logger, `log.With(..., "ts", log.DefaultTimestampUTC)` and `log.With(..., "caller", log.Caller(3))` build a go-kit context (`:72,28@v2.6.1`, `:193,37@v2.9.2`, `:186,44@v3.7.9`; `value.go:102@v0.2.1` RFC3339Nano UTC), and `level.Info(ctx)` prepends `level` to a context (`log.go:52-66,151-152@v0.2.1`), so the line reads `level=warn ts=2026-11-05T10:05:50.123456789Z caller=client.go:419 component=client host=loki:3100 msg="error sending batch, will retry" status=429 error="..."`. `-log.level` defaults to `info` (`vendor/github.com/weaveworks/common/logging/level.go:35-36@v2.6.1`, `vendor/github.com/grafana/dskit/log/level.go:36-37@v3.7.9`). `NOT_ERROR_RE` accepts `level=info ts=... msg="flushing stream" ...`; `ERROR_RE` takes `level=error ts=... msg="failed to flush" err="..."` and, through the word `error`, promtail's `level=warn ... msg="error sending batch, will retry" ...`.

| Signature | What kubegap relies on | Source | Holds for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `loki.rate-limited` | Loki answers 429 with `ingestion rate limit exceeded for user <id> (limit: N bytes/sec) ...` (capitalised `Ingestion` up to v3.5.x) or, per stream, with the ingester's `entry with timestamp <t> ignored, reason: 'Per stream rate limit exceeded (limit: ...) ...'` body | `pkg/validation/validate.go:23,63@v2.6.1`, `:23,74@v2.9.2`, `:26@v3.5.0`, `:30@v3.6.0`, `:30,91@v3.7.9`; `pkg/ingester/stream.go:327@v2.6.1`, `:255@v2.9.2`, `:278@v3.7.9` | all three; lower-case from v3.6.0 |
| | promtail logs the first line of that body in `error sending batch, will retry` (Warn, 429 and 5xx are retried) and `final error sending batch` (Error) as `error="server returned HTTP status 429 Too Many Requests (429): <first body line>"` (at most 1024 bytes); the reason phrase is Go's `Too Many Requests`; a dropped batch also logs `dropping batch due to rate limiting applied at ingester` (Warn, not at v2.6.1) | `clients/pkg/promtail/client/client.go:32,364,369,380,415@v2.6.1`, `:31,401,414,419,430,482@v2.9.2`, `:31,401,414,419,430,481@v3.7.9`; `src/net/http/status.go:179@go1.26.10` | all three |
| | Loki itself logs a rejected push at Debug only, both in the distributor handler and in the HTTP request log (4xx are Debug unless `-server.log-request-at-info-level-enabled`); the only Error line is the per-tenant opt-in `write operation failed` | `pkg/distributor/http.go:67@v2.6.1`, `:70@v2.9.2`, `:166@v3.7.9`; `vendor/github.com/weaveworks/common/middleware/logging.go:72-86@v2.6.1`, `vendor/github.com/grafana/dskit/middleware/logging.go:108-123@v2.9.2`, `:126-135@v3.7.9`; `pkg/distributor/writefailures/manager.go:42-49@v2.9.2`, `@v3.7.9` | all three, default level |
| | `too many outstanding requests` is the query frontend's 429 to Grafana; Loki logs it only in the Debug request log and promtail never sees it | `pkg/scheduler/queue/queue.go:20@v2.6.1`, `@v2.9.2`, `pkg/queue/queue.go:21@v3.7.9`; `pkg/lokifrontend/frontend/v1/frontend.go:29@v2.6.1`, `:29@v2.9.2`, `:32@v3.7.9` | all three |

| `loki.out-of-order` | The ingester's 400 body lists `entry with timestamp <t> ignored, reason: '<err>'` with `entry out of order` or `entry too far behind[, oldest acceptable timestamp is: ...]`; the distributor's 400 body says `entry for stream '<labels>' has timestamp too old: ...` or `... has timestamp too new: ...`; 400 is not retried, so promtail logs it once as `final error sending batch` | `pkg/chunkenc/interface.go:20,21@v2.6.1`, `:20,40@v2.9.2`, `:20,44@v3.7.9`; `pkg/validation/validate.go:38,41@v2.6.1`, `:47,50@v2.9.2`, `:54,57@v3.7.9`; `pkg/distributor/validator.go:75,81@v2.6.1`, `:76,83@v2.9.2`, `:106,112@v3.7.9`; `pkg/ingester/stream.go:327,331@v2.6.1`, `:255@v2.9.2`, `:278,282@v3.7.9` | all three |

| `loki.panic` | Go runtime texts `panic: `, `runtime error: `, `fatal error: ` | `src/runtime/panic.go:747,1224,1248@go1.26.10`, `src/runtime/error.go:92,103,185@go1.26.10` | all |
| `promtail.send-failed` | `error sending batch, will retry` (Warn, per retry) and `final error sending batch` (Error) with `server returned HTTP status 500 Internal Server Error (500): ...`, `connection refused` or `context deadline exceeded` inside `error=` | `clients/pkg/promtail/client/client.go:369,380,415@v2.6.1`, `:419,430,482@v2.9.2`, `:419,430,481@v3.7.9`; `src/net/http/status.go:185@go1.26.10`, `src/syscall/zerrors_linux_amd64.go:1466@go1.26.10`, `src/context/context.go:175@go1.26.10` | all three |
